Then \"Who's watching?\" appears with their own face on the profile. What loads next is an entire streaming homepage about them: your video playing behind the title, shelves of photo tiles that open into detail cards with your notes, a live countdown to the day that matters, and a closing letter that rolls as end credits. They scroll it like a real app, and then they screenshot it. That reaction is the whole point. Designed and animated by our team, so you just add names, photos, an optional video, and pick one of four cinematic themes. Ready in about a minute, yours for life, with a live link and QR code to share. One switch turns it from Girlfriend's Day to Friendship Day and the wording follows.
